SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

FILE: app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt
============================================================

     1	package com.prahlin.cinerific
     2	
     3	import android.app.UiModeManager
     4	import android.content.pm.ActivityInfo
     5	import android.graphics.Color
     6	import android.os.Build
     7	import android.os.Bundle
     8	import android.os.SystemClock
     9	import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
    10	import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
    11	import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
    12	import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
    13	import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat
    14	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.CinerificApp
    15	import com.prahlin.cinerific.ui.theme.CinerificTheme
    16	
    17	class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    18	    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
    19	        val bootStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
    20	        requestedOrientation = ActivityInfo.SCREEN_ORIENTATION_FULL_SENSOR
    21	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
    22	            getSystemService(UiModeManager::class.java).setApplicationNightMode(
    23	                UiModeManager.MODE_NIGHT_YES
    24	            )
    25	        }
    26	        enableEdgeToEdge(
    27	            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.TRANSPARENT),
    28	            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.dark(Color.BLACK),
    29	        )
    30	        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
    31	        window.statusBarColor = Color.TRANSPARENT
    32	        window.navigationBarColor = Color.BLACK
    33	        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.P) {
    34	            window.navigationBarDividerColor = Color.BLACK
    35	        }
    36	        WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, window.decorView).apply {
    37	            isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
    38	            isAppearanceLightNavigationBars = false
    39	        }
    40	        setContent {
    41	            CinerificTheme {
    42	                CinerificApp(bootStartMillis = bootStartMillis)
    43	            }
    44	        }
    45	    }
    46	}

    71	private const val FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT = 834f

    73	private const val SCREEN_PINK_MS = 1000

    76	private const val FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS = LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS
    77	private const val FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS = 4812
    78	private const val BOOT_ANIMATION_MS = FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS
    79	private const val AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000L

    81	private const val FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y = 92f

   122	@Composable
   123	fun CinerificApp(bootStartMillis: Long = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) {
   124	    val introBootStartMillis by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(bootStartMillis) }
   125	    var showHome by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   126	    var signedInProfile by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificProfile.Guest) }
   127	    var selectedLanguage by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificLanguage.English) }
   128	
   129	    if (showHome) {
   130	        CinerificLocalizedResources(selectedLanguage) {
   131	            CinerificMainExperience(
   132	                signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   133	                selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   134	                onLanguageSelected = { selectedLanguage = it },
   135	                onSignOut = {
   136	                    signedInProfile = CinerificProfile.Guest
   137	                    showHome = false
   138	                }
   139	            )
   140	        }
   141	    } else {
   142	        AndroidView(
   143	            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
   144	            factory = { context ->
   145	                CinerificIntroView(context).apply {
   146	                    this.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   147	                    onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   148	                        signedInProfile = profile
   149	                        showHome = true
   150	                    }
   151	                }
   152	            },
   153	            update = { view ->
   154	                view.bootStartMillis = introBootStartMillis
   155	                view.onAvatarSelected = { profile ->
   156	                    signedInProfile = profile
   157	                    showHome = true
   158	                }
   159	            }
   160	        )
   161	    }
   162	}
   163	
   164	internal enum class CinerificDestination {
   165	    Home,
   166	    Movies,
   167	    Shows,
   168	    Favorites,
   169	    Settings,
   170	    ProgramDetails
   171	}
   172	
   173	internal enum class CinerificProfile(
   174	    @DrawableRes val avatarResId: Int,
   175	    @DrawableRes val nameResId: Int
   176	) {
   177	    Steve(R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.steve_name),
   178	    Martin(R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.martin_name),
   179	    Janny(R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.janny_name),
   180	    Guest(R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test, R.drawable.guest_name)
   181	}
   182	
   183	@Composable
   184	private fun CinerificMainExperience(
   185	    signedInProfile: CinerificProfile,
   186	    selectedLanguage: CinerificLanguage,
   187	    onLanguageSelected: (CinerificLanguage) -> Unit,
   188	    onSignOut: () -> Unit
   189	) {
   190	    var destination by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(CinerificDestination.Home) }
   191	    var selectedProgramTitle by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("Sink or Swim") }
   192	    var favoriteProgramTitles by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyList<String>()) }
   193	    var userProgramRatings by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(emptyMap<String, Int>()) }
   194	    var catalogRouteDestinationName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   195	    var catalogRouteGenreName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   196	    var catalogRouteModeName by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
   197	    var favoritesFullPromptRequestId by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
   198	    var autoLogoutEnabled by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf(false) }
   199	    var userInitiatedPlaybackActive by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   200	    var lastInteractionMillis by remember { mutableStateOf(SystemClock.uptimeMillis()) }
   201	    val playbackSessionController = remember(userInitiatedPlaybackActive) {
   202	        CinerificPlaybackSessionController(
   203	            isUserInitiatedPlaybackActive = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   204	            onUserInitiatedPlaybackStarted = {
   205	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = true
   206	            },
   207	            finishUserInitiatedPlayback = {
   208	                userInitiatedPlaybackActive = false
   209	                lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   210	            }
   211	        )
   212	    }
   213	    val catalogRoute = remember(
   214	        catalogRouteDestinationName,
   215	        catalogRouteGenreName,
   216	        catalogRouteModeName
   217	    ) {
   218	        cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   219	            destinationName = catalogRouteDestinationName,
   220	            genreName = catalogRouteGenreName,
   221	            modeName = catalogRouteModeName
   222	        )
   223	    }
   224	
   225	    fun clearCatalogRoute() {
   226	        catalogRouteDestinationName = ""
   227	        catalogRouteGenreName = ""
   228	        catalogRouteModeName = ""
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    LaunchedEffect(autoLogoutEnabled, userInitiatedPlaybackActive, lastInteractionMillis) {
   232	        if (!autoLogoutEnabled || userInitiatedPlaybackActive) return@LaunchedEffect
   233	        delay(AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS)
   234	        val inactiveForMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - lastInteractionMillis
   235	        if (
   236	            autoLogoutEnabled &&
   237	            !userInitiatedPlaybackActive &&
   238	            inactiveForMillis >= AUTO_LOGOUT_TIMEOUT_MS
   239	        ) {
   240	            onSignOut()
   241	        }
   242	    }
   243	
   244	    fun showProgramDetails(title: String) {
   245	        clearCatalogRoute()
   246	        selectedProgramTitle = title
   247	        destination = CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails
   248	    }
   249	
   250	    fun showCatalog(route: CinerificCatalogRoute) {
   251	        catalogRouteDestinationName = route.destination.name
   252	        catalogRouteGenreName = route.genre.name
   253	        catalogRouteModeName = route.mode.name
   254	        destination = route.destination
   255	    }
   256	
   257	    fun rateProgram(title: String, rating: Int) {
   258	        userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings + (title to rating.coerceIn(1, 5))
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    fun toggleFavoriteProgram(title: String) {
   262	        if (title in favoriteProgramTitles) {
   263	            favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles - title
   264	            return
   265	        }
   266	
   267	        val isShow = cinerificProgramIsShow(title)
   268	        val filledSlots = cinerificFilledFavoritePlaceholderCount(
   269	            titles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   270	            isShow = isShow
   271	        )
   272	        if (filledSlots >= CINERIFIC_FAVORITES_CAPACITY) {
   273	            favoritesFullPromptRequestId += 1
   274	            return
   275	        }
   276	
   277	        favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles + title
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    CompositionLocalProvider(LocalCinerificPlaybackSessionController provides playbackSessionController) {
   281	        Box(
   282	            modifier = Modifier
   283	                .fillMaxSize()
   284	                .pointerInput(Unit) {
   285	                    awaitPointerEventScope {
   286	                        while (true) {
   287	                            awaitPointerEvent(PointerEventPass.Initial)
   288	                            lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   289	                        }
   290	                    }
   291	                }
   292	        ) {
   293	            when (destination) {
   294	                CinerificDestination.Home -> CinerificHomeScreen(
   295	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   296	                    onCatalogSelected = ::showCatalog,
   297	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   298	                )
   299	                CinerificDestination.Movies,
   300	                CinerificDestination.Shows,
   301	                CinerificDestination.Favorites,
   302	                CinerificDestination.Settings -> CinerificDestinationScreen(
   303	                    destination = destination,
   304	                    signedInProfile = signedInProfile,
   305	                    selectedLanguage = selectedLanguage,
   306	                    onLanguageSelected = onLanguageSelected,
   307	                    autoLogoutEnabled = autoLogoutEnabled,
   308	                    onAutoLogoutEnabledChange = { enabled ->
   309	                        autoLogoutEnabled = enabled
   310	                        lastInteractionMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   311	                    },
   312	                    onSignOut = onSignOut,
   313	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   314	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   315	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   316	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   317	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   318	                    catalogRoute = catalogRoute?.takeIf { it.destination == destination },
   319	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   320	                )
   321	                CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails -> CinerificProgramDetailsScreen(
   322	                    programTitle = selectedProgramTitle,
   323	                    favoriteProgramTitles = favoriteProgramTitles,
   324	                    onFavoriteToggled = ::toggleFavoriteProgram,
   325	                    userProgramRatings = userProgramRatings,
   326	                    onProgramRated = ::rateProgram,
   327	                    onProgramSelected = ::showProgramDetails,
   328	                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   329	                )
   330	            }
   331	
   332	            CinerificRightSideNavBar(
   333	                currentDestination = destination,
   334	                onDestinationSelected = {
   335	                    clearCatalogRoute()
   336	                    destination = it
   337	                },
   338	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   339	            )
   340	
   341	            FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   342	                requestId = favoritesFullPromptRequestId,
   343	                currentDestination = destination,
   344	                suppressed = userInitiatedPlaybackActive,
   345	                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
   346	            )
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	}
   350	
   351	private fun cinerificSavedCatalogRoute(
   352	    destinationName: String,
   353	    genreName: String,
   354	    modeName: String
   355	): CinerificCatalogRoute? {
   356	    if (destinationName.isBlank() || genreName.isBlank() || modeName.isBlank()) return null
   357	
   358	    val destination = CinerificDestination.values().firstOrNull { it.name == destinationName } ?: return null
   359	    val genre = ViewportGenre.values().firstOrNull { it.name == genreName } ?: return null
   360	    val mode = ViewportMode.values().firstOrNull { it.name == modeName } ?: return null
   361	
   362	    return CinerificCatalogRoute(
   363	        destination = destination,
   364	        genre = genre,
   365	        mode = mode
   366	    )
   367	}
   368	
   369	@Composable
   370	private fun FavoritesFullPromptOverlay(
   371	    requestId: Int,
   372	    currentDestination: CinerificDestination,
   373	    suppressed: Boolean,
   374	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   375	) {
   376	    var visible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
   377	
   378	    LaunchedEffect(requestId, suppressed) {
   379	        if (suppressed) {
   380	            visible = false
   381	            return@LaunchedEffect
   382	        }
   383	        if (requestId == 0) {
   384	            visible = false
   385	            return@LaunchedEffect
   386	        }
   387	        visible = true
   388	        delay(FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_VISIBLE_MS)
   389	        visible = false
   390	    }
   391	
   392	    val alpha by animateFloatAsState(
   393	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0f,
   394	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   395	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-alpha"
   396	    )
   397	    val promptScale by animateFloatAsState(
   398	        targetValue = if (visible) 1f else 0.94f,
   399	        animationSpec = tween(durationMillis = if (visible) 140 else 190),
   400	        label = "favorites-full-prompt-scale"
   401	    )
   402	
   403	    if (suppressed || requestId == 0 || (!visible && alpha <= 0.01f)) return
   404	
   405	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = modifier) {
   406	        val stageScale = maxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH
   407	        val isPortraitDetail = currentDestination == CinerificDestination.ProgramDetails &&
   408	            maxHeight > maxWidth
   409	        val promptY = if (isPortraitDetail) {
   410	            DETAIL_HERO_LOGO_CENTER_Y - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT / 2f
   411	        } else {
   412	            FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_Y
   413	        }
   414	        FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   415	            stageScale = stageScale,
   416	            modifier = Modifier
   417	                .absoluteOffset(
   418	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_X * stageScale).dp,
   419	                    y = (promptY * stageScale).dp
   420	                )
   421	                .graphicsLayer {
   422	                    this.alpha = alpha
   423	                    scaleX = promptScale
   424	                    scaleY = promptScale
   425	                    transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0.5f, 0f)
   426	                }
   427	        )
   428	    }
   429	}
   430	
   431	@Composable
   432	private fun FavoritesFullPromptBubble(
   433	    stageScale: Float,
   434	    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
   435	) {
   436	    Box(
   437	        modifier = modifier.requiredSize(
   438	            width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   439	            height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   440	        )
   441	    ) {
   442	        Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   443	            val xScale = size.width / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH
   444	            val yScale = size.height / FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_HEIGHT
   445	            val pointer = Path().apply {
   446	                moveTo(47.5f * xScale, 0f)
   447	                lineTo(63f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   448	                lineTo(32f * xScale, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale)
   449	                close()
   450	            }
   451	            drawPath(pointer, ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent)
   452	            drawRoundRect(
   453	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptFill,
   454	                topLeft = Offset(0f, FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale),
   455	                size = Size(
   456	                    width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH * xScale,
   457	                    height = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT * yScale
   458	                ),
   459	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   460	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   461	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   462	                )
   463	            )
   464	            drawRoundRect(
   465	                color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   466	                topLeft = Offset(
   467	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * xScale / 2f,
   468	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_TOP * yScale +
   469	                        FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * yScale / 2f
   470	                ),
   471	                size = Size(
   472	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_WIDTH - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * xScale,
   473	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_BOX_HEIGHT - FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE) * yScale
   474	                ),
   475	                cornerRadius = CornerRadius(
   476	                    x = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * xScale,
   477	                    y = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_RADIUS * yScale
   478	                ),
   479	                style = Stroke(width = FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_STROKE * min(xScale, yScale))
   480	            )
   481	        }
   482	        Text(
   483	            text = "You've\nfilled\nup on\nfavorites!",
   484	            color = ColorFavoritesFullPromptAccent,
   485	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   486	            fontSize = (14f * stageScale).sp,
   487	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   488	            lineHeight = (21.252f * stageScale).sp,
   489	            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   490	            modifier = Modifier
   491	                .absoluteOffset(
   492	                    x = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_X * stageScale).dp,
   493	                    y = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_Y * stageScale).dp
   494	                )
   495	                .requiredSize(
   496	                    width = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_WIDTH * stageScale).dp,
   497	                    height = (FAVORITES_FULL_PROMPT_TEXT_HEIGHT * stageScale).dp
   498	                )
   499	        )
   500	    }
   501	}
   502	
   503	@Composable
   504	private fun BootIntroFromFigma(progress: Float) {
   505	    val p = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   506	    val images = rememberIntroImages()
   507	    val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   508	        p,
   509	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   510	        SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   511	    )
   512	    val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(p, 2500, 3900)
   513	    val background = introBackgroundBrush(
   514	        solidProgress = blackToPurple,
   515	        gradientProgress = purpleToSettle
   516	    )
   517	    val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(p, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   518	    val logoEntryProgress = bouncySegmentMs(p, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   519	    val logoFinalProgress = easedSegmentMs(p, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   520	    val avatarSettleAlpha = easedSegmentMs(p, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   521	    val avatarYOffset = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarSettleAlpha)
   522	
   523	    Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
   524	        val stageScale = min(size.width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, size.height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   525	        val stageLeft = (size.width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f
   526	        val stageTop = (size.height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f
   527	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(size.width > size.height)
   528	
   529	        drawRect(brush = background, size = size)
   530	
   531	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   532	            val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   533	            val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   534	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   535	                end = baseBounds,
   536	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   537	            )
   538	            val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   539	                start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   540	                end = baseBounds,
   541	                amount = logoEntryProgress
   542	            )
   543	            val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   544	
   545	            drawFigmaImage(
   546	                image = images.logoSimple,
   547	                bounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   548	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   549	                stageTop = stageTop,
   550	                stageScale = stageScale,
   551	                alpha = logoAlpha
   552	            )
   553	            drawFigmaImage(
   554	                image = images.logoEyes,
   555	                bounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, logoFinalProgress),
   556	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
   557	                stageTop = stageTop,
   558	                stageScale = stageScale,
   559	                alpha = logoAlpha
   560	            )
   561	        }
   562	
   563	        if (avatarSettleAlpha > 0.01f) {
   564	            val y = avatarYOffset
   565	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveAvatar, FigmaBounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   566	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinAvatar, FigmaBounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   567	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyAvatar, FigmaBounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   568	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestAvatar, FigmaBounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha, clipCircle = true)
   569	
   570	            drawFigmaImage(images.steveName, FigmaBounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   571	            drawFigmaImage(images.martinName, FigmaBounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   572	            drawFigmaImage(images.jannyName, FigmaBounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   573	            drawFigmaImage(images.guestName, FigmaBounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, avatarSettleAlpha)
   574	        }
   575	    }
   576	}

   600	}

   769	
   770	private fun DrawScope.drawFigmaImage(
   771	    image: ImageBitmap,
   772	    bounds: FigmaBounds,
   773	    stageLeft: Float,
   774	    stageTop: Float,
   775	    stageScale: Float,
   776	    alpha: Float = 1f,
   777	    clipCircle: Boolean = false
   778	) {
   779	    val left = stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale
   780	    val top = stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale
   781	    val width = bounds.w * stageScale
   782	    val height = bounds.h * stageScale
   783	
   784	    if (clipCircle) {
   785	        val clip = Path().apply {
   786	            addOval(Rect(left, top, left + width, top + height))
   787	        }
   788	        clipPath(clip) {
   789	            drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   790	        }
   791	    } else {
   792	        drawImageBitmap(image, left, top, width, height, alpha)
   793	    }
   794	}
   795	
   796	private fun DrawScope.drawImageBitmap(
   797	    image: ImageBitmap,
   798	    left: Float,
   799	    top: Float,
   800	    width: Float,
   801	    height: Float,
   802	    alpha: Float
   803	) {
   804	    drawImage(
   805	        image = image,
   806	        dstOffset = IntOffset(left.roundToInt(), top.roundToInt()),
   807	        dstSize = IntSize(width.roundToInt(), height.roundToInt()),
   808	        alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   809	    )
   810	}

   812	@Composable
   813	private fun SettlingLogoLayer(
   814	    scale: Float,
   815	    entryProgress: Float,
   816	    finalProgress: Float,
   817	    alpha: Float
   818	) {
   819	    val baseBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 222f, y = 150f, w = 750f, h = 535f)
   820	    val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(
   821	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 447f, y = 1037f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   822	        end = baseBounds,
   823	        amount = entryProgress
   824	    )
   825	    val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(
   826	        start = FigmaBounds(x = 453f, y = -417f, w = 300f, h = 214f),
   827	        end = baseBounds,
   828	        amount = entryProgress
   829	    )
   830	    val finalBounds = FigmaBounds(x = 297f, y = 11f, w = 600f, h = 428f)
   831	    val simpleBounds = lerpBounds(simpleEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   832	    val eyesBounds = lerpBounds(eyesEntry, finalBounds, finalProgress)
   833	
   834	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   835	        base = baseBounds,
   836	        target = simpleBounds,
   837	        scale = scale,
   838	        resId = R.drawable.logo_simple_large,
   839	        alpha = alpha
   840	    )
   841	    TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
   842	        base = baseBounds,
   843	        target = eyesBounds,
   844	        scale = scale,
   845	        resId = R.drawable.logo_eyes_large,
   846	        alpha = alpha
   847	    )
   848	}

   850	@Composable
   851	private fun AvatarSelectionLayer(scale: Float, alpha: Float, yOffset: Float) {
   852	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
   853	    val configuration = LocalConfiguration.current
   854	    val isLandscape = configuration.screenWidthDp > configuration.screenHeightDp
   855	    val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY(isLandscape)
   856	    val accountPromptExtraShiftY = signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape)
   857	    val accountPromptTextSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape)
   858	    val accountPromptSecondaryTextSize = accountPromptTextSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   859	    Box(
   860	        modifier = Modifier
   861	            .requiredSize(figma(1194f, scale), figma(834f, scale))
   862	            .graphicsLayer {
   863	                this.alpha = alpha
   864	                translationY = yOffset * scale * density
   865	            }
   866	    ) {
   867	        // Figma Logo Slide 2, Property 1=Variant2, constrained inside Sign-in Frame.
   868	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 152f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   869	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 390f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   870	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 628f, y = 454f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   871	        FigmaAvatarImage(x = 866f, y = 450f + stackShiftY, w = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, h = SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_avatar_bubble_edge50_body0_test)
   872	
   873	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 130f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.steve_name)
   874	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 368f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.martin_name)
   875	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 606f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 220f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.janny_name)
   876	        FigmaAssetImage(x = 854f, y = SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY, w = 200f, h = 72f, scale = scale, resId = R.drawable.guest_name)
   877	        SignInPromptText(
   878	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   879	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X,
   880	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   881	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   882	            textAlign = TextAlign.Left,
   883	            scale = scale
   884	        )
   885	        SignInPromptText(
   886	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   887	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH / 2f,
   888	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   889	            textSize = accountPromptTextSize,
   890	            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
   891	            scale = scale
   892	        )
   893	        SignInPromptText(
   894	            text = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   895	            x = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X - ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH,
   896	            y = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TOP + stackShiftY + accountPromptExtraShiftY,
   897	            textSize = accountPromptSecondaryTextSize,
   898	            textAlign = TextAlign.Right,
   899	            scale = scale
   900	        )
   901	    }
   902	}
   903	
   904	@Composable
   905	private fun SignInPromptText(
   906	    text: String,
   907	    x: Float,
   908	    y: Float,
   909	    textSize: Float,
   910	    fontWeight: FontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
   911	    textAlign: TextAlign = TextAlign.Center,
   912	    scale: Float
   913	) {
   914	    val contentAlignment = when (textAlign) {
   915	        TextAlign.Left -> Alignment.CenterStart
   916	        TextAlign.Right -> Alignment.CenterEnd
   917	        else -> Alignment.Center
   918	    }
   919	    Box(
   920	        modifier = Modifier
   921	            .absoluteOffset(
   922	                x = figma(x, scale),
   923	                y = figma(y, scale)
   924	            )
   925	            .requiredSize(
   926	                width = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_WIDTH, scale),
   927	                height = figma(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_HEIGHT, scale)
   928	            ),
   929	        contentAlignment = contentAlignment
   930	    ) {
   931	        Text(
   932	            text = text,
   933	            color = Color.White,
   934	            fontFamily = CinerificAppTextFontFamily,
   935	            fontSize = (textSize * scale).sp,
   936	            fontWeight = fontWeight,
   937	            lineHeight = (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LINE_HEIGHT * textSize / ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * scale).sp,
   938	            maxLines = 1,
   939	            softWrap = false,
   940	            textAlign = textAlign
   941	        )
   942	    }
   943	}

   945	@Composable
   946	private fun FigmaAvatarImage(
   947	    x: Float,
   948	    y: Float,
   949	    w: Float,
   950	    h: Float,
   951	    scale: Float,
   952	    @DrawableRes resId: Int
   953	) {
   954	    Image(
   955	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
   956	        contentDescription = null,
   957	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
   958	        modifier = Modifier
   959	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
   960	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
   961	            .clip(CircleShape)
   962	    )
   963	}

   972	            .background(if (active) Color(0xFFE7E7E7) else Color(0xFF050505))
   973	    )
   974	}

   976	@Composable
   977	private fun PromoBackgroundLayer(
   978	    scale: Float,
   979	    darkOverlay: Float,
   980	    @DrawableRes backgroundResId: Int,
   981	    alpha: Float = 1f
   982	) {
   983	    // Nodes 793:7049 / 793:7050 at x:0 y:0 size:1584x1584.
   984	    FigmaAssetImage(
   985	        x = 0f,
   986	        y = 0f,
   987	        w = 1584f,
   988	        h = 1584f,
   989	        scale = scale,
   990	        resId = backgroundResId,
   991	        alpha = alpha
   992	    )
   993	
   994	    if (darkOverlay > 0f) {
   995	        Box(
   996	            modifier = Modifier
   997	                .absoluteOffset(x = figma(0f, scale), y = figma(0f, scale))
   998	                .requiredSize(figma(1584f, scale), figma(1584f, scale))
   999	                .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1000	                .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = darkOverlay))
  1001	        )
  1002	    }
  1003	}
  1004	
  1005	@Composable
  1006	private fun TransformedFigmaAssetImage(
  1007	    base: FigmaBounds,
  1008	    target: FigmaBounds,
  1009	    scale: Float,
  1010	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1011	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1012	) {
  1013	    val density = LocalDensity.current.density
  1014	    Image(
  1015	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1016	        contentDescription = null,
  1017	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1018	        modifier = Modifier
  1019	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(base.x, scale), y = figma(base.y, scale))
  1020	            .requiredSize(figma(base.w, scale), figma(base.h, scale))
  1021	            .graphicsLayer {
  1022	                this.alpha = alpha
  1023	                transformOrigin = TransformOrigin(0f, 0f)
  1024	                translationX = (target.x - base.x) * scale * density
  1025	                translationY = (target.y - base.y) * scale * density
  1026	                scaleX = target.w / base.w
  1027	                scaleY = target.h / base.h
  1028	            }
  1029	    )
  1030	}
  1031	
  1032	@Composable
  1033	private fun FigmaAssetImage(
  1034	    x: Float,
  1035	    y: Float,
  1036	    w: Float,
  1037	    h: Float,
  1038	    scale: Float,
  1039	    @DrawableRes resId: Int,
  1040	    alpha: Float = 1f
  1041	) {
  1042	    Image(
  1043	        painter = painterResource(id = resId),
  1044	        contentDescription = null,
  1045	        contentScale = ContentScale.FillBounds,
  1046	        modifier = Modifier
  1047	            .absoluteOffset(x = figma(x, scale), y = figma(y, scale))
  1048	            .requiredSize(figma(w, scale), figma(h, scale))
  1049	            .graphicsLayer(alpha = alpha)
  1050	    )
  1051	}
  1052	
  1053	@Composable
  1054	private fun FigmaStage(background: Brush, content: @Composable BoxWithConstraintsScope.(Float) -> Unit) {
  1055	    BoxWithConstraints(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  1056	        val outerMaxWidth = this.maxWidth
  1057	        val outerMaxHeight = this.maxHeight
  1058	        val scale = min(outerMaxWidth.value / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, outerMaxHeight.value / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  1059	        val stageWidth = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, scale)
  1060	        val stageHeight = figma(FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT, scale)
  1061	
  1062	        Box(
  1063	            modifier = Modifier
  1064	                .fillMaxSize()
  1065	                .background(background)
  1066	        ) {
  1067	            BoxWithConstraints(
  1068	                modifier = Modifier
  1069	                    .align(Alignment.Center)
  1070	                    .requiredSize(stageWidth, stageHeight)
  1071	            ) {
  1072	                content(scale)
  1073	            }
  1074	        }
  1075	    }
  1076	}
  1077	
  1078	private fun figma(px: Float, scale: Float) = (px * scale).dp
  1079	
  1080	private fun signInStackShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1081	    return if (isLandscape) {
  1082	        SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1083	    } else {
  1084	        SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
  1085	    }
  1086	}
  1087	
  1088	private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1089	    return if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1090	}
  1091	
  1092	private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(isLandscape: Boolean): Float {
  1093	    return if (isLandscape) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
  1094	}
  1095	
  1096	private fun introBackgroundBrush(solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float): Brush {
  1097	    val solid = lerpColor(ColorFrame1Background, ColorFrame2Background, solidProgress)
  1098	    return Brush.verticalGradient(
  1099	        colors = listOf(
  1100	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientTop, gradientProgress),
  1101	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientCenter, gradientProgress),
  1102	            lerpColor(solid, ColorIntroGradientBottom, gradientProgress)
  1103	        )
  1104	    )
  1105	}
  1106	
  1107	private fun solidBrush(color: Color): Brush = Brush.verticalGradient(
  1108	    colors = listOf(color, color)
  1109	)
  1110	
  1111	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1112	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1113	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1114	}
  1115	
  1116	private fun easedSegment(value: Float, start: Float, end: Float): Float {
  1117	    val progress = ((value - start) / (end - start)).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1118	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(progress)
  1119	}
  1120	
  1121	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1122	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1123	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1124	}
  1125	
  1126	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1127	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1128	}
  1129	
  1130	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1131	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1132	}
  1133	
  1134	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1135	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1136	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1137	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1138	    return base + bounce
  1139	}
  1140	
  1141	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1142	
  1143	private fun lerpBounds(start: FigmaBounds, end: FigmaBounds, amount: Float) = FigmaBounds(
  1144	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1145	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1146	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1147	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1148	)
