   197	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   198	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   199	
   200	        val progress = bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis)
   201	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   202	            progress,
   203	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   204	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   205	        )
   206	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   207	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   208	
   209	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   210	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   211	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   212	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   213	
   214	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   215	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   216	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha, formFocusMotion)
   217	        }
   218	
   219	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   220	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   221	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   222	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   223	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   224	            val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   225	            if (stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   226	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveAvatar, Bounds(152f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   227	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   228	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   229	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestAvatar, Bounds(866f, 450f + stackShiftY + y, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   230	
   231	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, steveName, Bounds(130f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   232	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   233	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 220f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   234	                drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, guestName, Bounds(854f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + y, 200f, 72f), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, stackAlpha)
   235	                drawAccountPrompt(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, y, stackAlpha)
   236	            }
   237	        }
   238	        if (mockProgress > 0f) {
   239	            drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   240	        }
   241	
   242	        if (progress < 1f || isMockSignInAnimating() || isMockFormLabelAnimating() || isMockFormFocusAnimating()) {
   243	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   244	        }
   245	    }
   246	
   247	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   248	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   249	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   250	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   251	                    pressedMockField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   252	                    return true
   253	                }
   254	                pressedSignInPrompt = settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   255	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (pressedSignInPrompt) {
   256	                    null
   257	                } else {
   258	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   259	                }
   260	                pressedSignInPrompt || pressedAvatarProfile != null
   261	            }
   262	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   263	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   264	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   265	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
   266	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
   267	                    pressedMockField = null
   268	                    super.performClick()
   269	                    if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
   270	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
   271	                    } else {
   272	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
   273	                    }
   274	                    return true
   275	                }
   276	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = pressedSignInPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   277	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   278	                val shouldNavigate = pressedAvatarProfile != null && pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
   279	                clickAvatarProfile = pressedAvatarProfile
   280	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   281	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   282	                if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
   283	                    super.performClick()
   284	                    openMockSignInScreen()
   285	                    true
   286	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
   287	                    performClick()
   288	                    true
   289	                } else {
   290	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
   291	                    false
   292	                }
   293	            }
   294	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
   295	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
   296	                clickAvatarProfile = null
   297	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
   298	                pressedMockField = null
   299	                false
   300	            }
   301	            else -> pressedSignInPrompt || pressedAvatarProfile != null || mockSignInStartMillis != null
   302	        }
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
   306	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
   310	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
   311	        outAttrs.inputType = if (field == MockSignInField.Password) {
   312	            InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
   313	        } else {
   314	            InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
   315	        }
   316	        outAttrs.imeOptions = if (field == MockSignInField.Username) {
   317	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
   318	        } else {
   319	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
   320	        }
   321	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
   322	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
   323	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
   324	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   328	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
   329	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   330	        }
   331	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
   335	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   336	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
   337	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
   338	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
   339	        }
   340	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
   344	        super.performClick()
   345	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   346	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
   347	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
   348	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   349	        return true
   350	    }
   351	
   352	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
   353	        canvas: Canvas,
   354	        stageLeft: Float,
   355	        stageTop: Float,
   356	        stageScale: Float,
   357	        progress: Float,
   358	        alpha: Float,
   359	        formFocusMotion: Float
   360	    ) {
   361	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
   362	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   363	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
   364	        val base = offsetBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), focusShiftY)
   365	        val final = offsetBounds(Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT), focusShiftY)
   366	
   367	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
   368	            drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoCombined, lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   369	            return
   370	        }
   371	
   372	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   373	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
   374	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   375	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   376	    }
   377	
   378	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
   379	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
   380	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
   381	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
   382	            return
   383	        }
   384	
   385	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
   386	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
   387	            0f,
   388	            0f,
   389	            0f,
   390	            height.toFloat(),
   391	            intArrayOf(
   392	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
   393	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
   394	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
   395	            ),
   396	            null,
   397	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
   398	        )
   399	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   400	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
   401	
   402	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
   403	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
   404	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
   405	        }
   406	    }
   407	
   408	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
   409	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
   410	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   411	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
   412	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
   413	    }
   414	
   415	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
   416	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
   417	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
   418	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
   419	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
   420	    }
   421	
   422	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
   423	        canvas: Canvas,
   424	        bitmap: Bitmap,
   425	        bounds: Bounds,
   426	        stageLeft: Float,
   427	        stageTop: Float,
   428	        stageScale: Float,
   429	        alpha: Float
   430	    ) {
   431	        tempRect.set(
   432	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
   433	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
   434	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
   435	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
   436	        )
   437	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   438	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
   439	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
   440	    }
   441	
   442	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
   443	        canvas: Canvas,
   444	        stageLeft: Float,
   445	        stageTop: Float,
   446	        stageScale: Float,
   447	        yOffset: Float,
   448	        alpha: Float
   449	    ) {
   450	        val isLandscape = width > height
   451	        val textSize = signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
   452	        val secondaryTextSize = textSize * ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SECONDARY_TEXT_SCALE
   453	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   454	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = secondaryTextSize
   455	        accountPromptBoldPaint.textSize = textSize
   456	        accountPromptBoldPaint.strokeWidth = 0.65f * stageScale
   457	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
   458	        accountPromptBoldPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
   459	
   460	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
   461	        val centerY = stageTop +
   462	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset) * stageScale
   463	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
   464	        val primaryMetrics = accountPromptBoldPaint.fontMetrics
   465	        val secondaryBaselineY = centerY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
   466	        val primaryBaselineY = centerY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
   467	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
   468	        canvas.drawText(
   469	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
   470	            stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LEFT_ANCHOR_X * stageScale,
   471	            secondaryBaselineY,
   472	            accountPromptPaint
   473	        )
   474	        accountPromptBoldPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   475	        canvas.drawText(
   476	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
   477	            stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale,
   478	            primaryBaselineY,
   479	            accountPromptBoldPaint
   480	        )
   481	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.RIGHT
   482	        canvas.drawText(
   483	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
   484	            stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_RIGHT_ANCHOR_X * stageScale,
   485	            secondaryBaselineY,
   486	            accountPromptPaint
   487	        )
   488	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
   489	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
   490	        accountPromptBoldPaint.alpha = 255
   491	    }
   492	
   493	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
   494	        canvas: Canvas,
   495	        stageLeft: Float,
   496	        stageTop: Float,
   497	        stageScale: Float,
   498	        progress: Float,
   499	        formFocusMotion: Float
   500	    ) {
   501	        updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
   502	
   503	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   504	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
   505	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
   506	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
   507	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
   508	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset
   509	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
   510	
   511	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
   512	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
   513	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
   514	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
   515	
   516	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
   517	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   518	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
   519	
   520	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
   521	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
   522	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
   523	    }
   524	
   525	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
   526	        canvas: Canvas,
   527	        field: MockSignInField,
   528	        x: Float,
   529	        y: Float,
   530	        stageLeft: Float,
   531	        stageTop: Float,
   532	        stageScale: Float,
   533	        alpha: Float
   534	    ) {
   535	        tempRect.set(
   536	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
   537	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
   538	            stageLeft + (x + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stageScale,
   539	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stageScale
   540	        )
   541	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * stageScale
   542	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
   543	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
   544	
   545	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
   546	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
   547	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
   548	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
   549	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
   550	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
   551	            labelProgress
   552	        ) * stageScale
   553	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
   554	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
   555	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
   556	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
   557	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * stageScale - metrics.ascent
   558	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
   559	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale,
   560	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * stageScale,
   561	            labelProgress
   562	        )
   563	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
   564	        canvas.drawText(
   565	            label,
   566	            labelX,
   567	            labelBaselineY,
   568	            formLabelPaint
   569	        )
   570	
   571	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
   572	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
   573	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
   574	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
   575	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
   576	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
   577	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
   578	
   579	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale
   580	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top + MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * stageScale
   581	            val maxInputWidth = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
   582	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
   583	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
   584	
   585	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
   586	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) + 2f * stageScale
   587	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
   588	                canvas.drawLine(
   589	                    caretX,
   590	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
   591	                    caretX,
   592	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
   593	                    formCaretPaint
   594	                )
   595	            }
   596	        }
   597	    }
   598	
   599	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {
   600	        return BitmapFactory.decodeResource(resources, resId, bitmapOptions)
   601	    }
   602	
   603	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
   604	        return when {
   605	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
   606	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
   607	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
   608	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
   609	            else -> null
   610	        }
   611	    }
   612	
   613	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
   614	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
   615	    }
   616	
   617	    private fun isFinalFrameSettled(): Boolean {
   618	        return bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis) >= 1f
   619	    }
   620	
   621	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
   622	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
   623	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
   624	
   625	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
   626	
   627	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
   628	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
   629	            val bounds = target.bounds
   630	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
   631	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + stackShiftY + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
   632	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
   633	            val dx = x - centerX
   634	            val dy = y - centerY
   635	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
   636	        }?.profile
   637	    }
   638	
   639	    private fun settledSignInPromptHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
   640	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null || !isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
   641	
   642	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
   643	        val centerX = stage.left + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stage.scale
   644	        val centerY = stage.top +
   645	            (ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()) * stage.scale
   646	        val halfWidth = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
   647	        val halfHeight = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_HIT_HEIGHT * stage.scale / 2f
   648	        return x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) &&
   649	            y in (centerY - halfHeight)..(centerY + halfHeight)
   650	    }
   651	
   652	    private fun mockSignInFieldHit(x: Float, y: Float): MockSignInField? {
   653	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null) return null
   654	
   655	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
   656	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
   657	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
   658	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
   659	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
   660	        val usernameY = MOCK_FORM_Y + yOffset + focusShiftY
   661	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_GAP
   662	        return when {
   663	            mockSignInFieldContains(x, y, fieldX, usernameY, stage) -> MockSignInField.Username
   664	            mockSignInFieldContains(x, y, fieldX, passwordY, stage) -> MockSignInField.Password
   665	            else -> null
   666	        }
   667	    }
   668	
   669	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
   670	        pointerX: Float,
   671	        pointerY: Float,
   672	        fieldX: Float,
   673	        fieldY: Float,
   674	        stage: StageMetrics
   675	    ): Boolean {
   676	        val left = stage.left + fieldX * stage.scale
   677	        val top = stage.top + fieldY * stage.scale
   678	        val right = stage.left + (fieldX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) * stage.scale
   679	        val bottom = stage.top + (fieldY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) * stage.scale
   680	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
   681	    }
   682	
   683	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
   684	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
   685	            activeComposingText = ""
   686	            focusedMockField = field
   687	            restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   688	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   689	        }
   690	        requestFocus()
   691	        post {
   692	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
   693	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
   694	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
   695	        }
   696	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   697	    }
   698	
   699	    private fun clearMockSignInFieldFocus() {
   700	        if (focusedMockField == null) return
   701	        activeComposingText = ""
   702	        focusedMockField = null
   703	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   704	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   705	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
   706	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
   707	        clearFocus()
   708	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   709	    }
   710	
   711	    private fun mockFormX(): Float {
   712	        return (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - MOCK_FORM_WIDTH) / 2f
   713	    }
   714	
   715	    private fun updateMockFormLabelAnimation() {
   716	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   717	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormLabelAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
   718	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = now
   719	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()
   720	        usernameLabelFloatProgress = moveToward(
   721	            usernameLabelFloatProgress,
   722	            mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(MockSignInField.Username),
   723	            step
   724	        )
   725	        passwordLabelFloatProgress = moveToward(
   726	            passwordLabelFloatProgress,
   727	            mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(MockSignInField.Password),
   728	            step
   729	        )
   730	    }
   731	
   732	    private fun updateMockFormFocusAnimation() {
   733	        val now = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   734	        val elapsed = (now - lastFormFocusAnimationMillis).coerceIn(0L, 48L)
   735	        lastFormFocusAnimationMillis = now
   736	        val step = elapsed / MOCK_FORM_FOCUS_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()
   737	        mockFormFocusProgress = moveToward(mockFormFocusProgress, mockFormFocusTarget(), step)
   738	    }
   739	
   740	    private fun isMockFormLabelAnimating(): Boolean {
   741	        return usernameLabelFloatProgress != mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(MockSignInField.Username) ||
   742	            passwordLabelFloatProgress != mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(MockSignInField.Password)
   743	    }
   744	
   745	    private fun isMockFormFocusAnimating(): Boolean {
   746	        return mockFormFocusProgress != mockFormFocusTarget()
   747	    }
   748	
   749	    private fun restartMockFormLabelAnimation() {
   750	        lastFormLabelAnimationMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - 16L
   751	    }
   752	
   753	    private fun restartMockFormFocusAnimation() {
   754	        lastFormFocusAnimationMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - 16L
   755	    }
   756	
   757	    private fun mockFormFocusTarget(): Float {
   758	        return if (
   759	            focusedMockField != null ||
   760	            usernameText.isNotEmpty() ||
   761	            passwordText.isNotEmpty()
   762	        ) {
   763	            1f
   764	        } else {
   765	            0f
   766	        }
   767	    }
   768	
   769	    private fun mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion: Float): Float {
   770	        return MOCK_FORM_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * formFocusMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   771	    }
   772	
   773	    private fun mockLogoFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion: Float): Float {
   774	        val focusMotion = formFocusMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   775	        return if (width > height) {
   776	            -(LOGO_FINAL_TOP + LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT + 24f) * focusMotion
   777	        } else {
   778	            mockFormFocusShiftY(focusMotion)
   779	        }
   780	    }
   781	
   782	    private fun mockSignInFieldLabelTarget(field: MockSignInField): Float {
   783	        return if (focusedMockField == field || mockSignInFieldText(field).isNotEmpty()) 1f else 0f
   784	    }
   785	
   786	    private fun mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field: MockSignInField): Float {
   787	        return when (field) {
   788	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameLabelFloatProgress
   789	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordLabelFloatProgress
   790	        }
   791	    }
   792	
   793	    private fun mockSignInFieldLabel(field: MockSignInField): String {
   794	        return when (field) {
   795	            MockSignInField.Username -> MOCK_FORM_USERNAME_TEXT
   796	            MockSignInField.Password -> MOCK_FORM_PASSWORD_TEXT
   797	        }
   798	    }
   799	
   800	    private fun mockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField): String {
   801	        return when (field) {
   802	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
   803	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText
   804	        }
   805	    }
   806	
   807	    private fun mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field: MockSignInField): String {
   808	        return when (field) {
   809	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText
   810	            MockSignInField.Password -> "*".repeat(passwordText.length)
   811	        }
   812	    }
   813	
   814	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
   815	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
   816	        when (field) {
   817	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
   818	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
   819	        }
   820	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   821	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   822	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   823	    }
   824	
   825	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
   826	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
   827	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
   828	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
   829	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
   830	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
   831	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
   832	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
   833	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
   834	        activeComposingText = ""
   835	        return true
   836	    }
   837	
   838	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
   839	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
   840	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
   841	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
   842	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
   843	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
   844	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
   845	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
   846	        activeComposingText = composingText
   847	        return true
   848	    }
   849	
   850	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
   851	        activeComposingText = ""
   852	        return true
   853	    }
   854	
   855	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
   856	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
   857	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
   858	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
   859	            activeComposingText = ""
   860	            return true
   861	        }
   862	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
   863	        activeComposingText = ""
   864	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
   865	        return true
   866	    }
   867	
   868	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
   869	        activeComposingText = ""
   870	        if (focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Username && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
   871	            focusMockSignInField(MockSignInField.Password)
   872	        } else {
   873	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
   874	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
   875	        }
   876	        return true
   877	    }
   878	
   879	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
   880	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
   881	        return when (keyCode) {
   882	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
   883	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
   884	            else -> {
   885	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
   886	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
   887	            }
   888	        }
   889	    }
   890	
   891	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
   892	        return buildString {
   893	            input.forEach { character ->
   894	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
   895	                    append(character)
   896	                }
   897	            }
   898	        }
   899	    }
   900	
   901	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
   902	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
   903	
   904	        var startIndex = 0
   905	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
   906	            startIndex += 1
   907	        }
   908	        return text.substring(startIndex)
   909	    }
   910	
   911	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
   912	        return when {
   913	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
   914	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
   915	            else -> current
   916	        }
   917	    }
   918	
   919	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
   920	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
   921	
   922	        val stageScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
   923	        return StageMetrics(
   924	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
   925	            top = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * stageScale) / 2f,
   926	            scale = stageScale
   927	        )
   928	    }
   929	
   930	    private fun signInStackShiftY(): Float {
   931	        return if (width > height) {
   932	            SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_STACK_SHIFT_Y
   933	        } else {
   934	            SIGN_IN_PORTRAIT_STACK_SHIFT_Y
   935	        }
   936	    }
   937	
   938	    private fun signInAccountPromptTextSize(): Float {
   939	        return if (width > height) ACCOUNT_PROMPT_LANDSCAPE_TEXT_SIZE else ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE
   940	    }
   941	
   942	    private fun signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY(): Float {
   943	        return if (width > height) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
   944	    }
   945	
   946	    private fun openMockSignInScreen() {
   947	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return
   948	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   949	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   950	    }
   951	
   952	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
   953	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
   954	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
   955	        return (elapsed / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
   956	    }
   957	
   958	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
   959	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
   960	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS
   961	    }
   962	
   963	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
   964	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
   965	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
   966	        }
   967	
   968	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
   969	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
   970	        }
   971	
   972	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
   973	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
   974	        }
   975	
   976	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
   977	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
   978	        }
   979	
   980	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
   981	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
   982	        }
   983	
   984	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
   985	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
   986	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
   987	        }
   988	
   989	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
   990	            return ""
   991	        }
   992	
   993	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
   994	            return ""
   995	        }
   996	
   997	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
   998	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
   999	        }
  1000	
  1001	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1002	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  1003	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  1004	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  1005	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  1006	                else -> {
  1007	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  1008	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  1009	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  1010	                    } else {
  1011	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  1012	                    }
  1013	                }
  1014	            }
  1015	        }
  1016	    }
  1017	}
  1018	
  1019	private enum class MockSignInField {
  1020	    Username,
  1021	    Password
  1022	}
  1023	
  1024	private data class AvatarTarget(
  1025	    val profile: CinerificProfile,
  1026	    val bounds: Bounds
  1027	)
  1028	
  1029	private data class StageMetrics(
  1030	    val left: Float,
  1031	    val top: Float,
  1032	    val scale: Float
  1033	)
  1034	
  1035	private data class Bounds(
  1036	    val x: Float,
  1037	    val y: Float,
  1038	    val w: Float,
  1039	    val h: Float
  1040	)
  1041	
  1042	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  1043	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  1044	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1045	}
  1046	
  1047	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1048	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  1049	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1050	}
  1051	
  1052	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1053	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1054	}
  1055	
  1056	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  1057	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  1058	}
  1059	
  1060	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  1061	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1062	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  1063	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  1064	    return base + bounce
  1065	}
  1066	
  1067	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  1068	
  1069	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  1070	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  1071	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  1072	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  1073	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  1074	)
  1075	
  1076	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  1077	    x = bounds.x,
  1078	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  1079	    w = bounds.w,
  1080	    h = bounds.h
  1081	)
  1082	
  1083	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  1084	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1085	    return Color.argb(
  1086	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  1087	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  1088	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  1089	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  1090	    )
  1091	}
  1092	
  1093	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  1094	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  1095	}
  1096	
  1097	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  1098	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  1099	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  1100	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  1101	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  1102	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  1103	    return output
  1104	}
  1105	
  1106	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  1107	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  1108	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  1109	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  1110	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  1111	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  1112	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  1113	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  1114	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  1115	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  1116	    return output
  1117	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    32	
    33	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    34	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    35	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   177	  do
   178	    print_file "$file"
   179	  done
   180	
   181	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   182	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   183	
   184	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   185	
   186	
   187	============================================================
   188	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   189	============================================================
   190	
   191	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   192	- .git
   193	- .gradle
   194	- .idea
   195	- app/build
   196	- build
   197	- .kotlin
   198	- .tmp
   199	- .cache
   200	- .DS_Store
   201	- local.properties
   202	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   203	- *.jks
   204	- *.keystore
   205	
   206	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	}
   210	
   211	write_snapshot
   212	
   213	echo "Done."
   214	echo ""
   215	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   216	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Open it with:"
   219	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
